critical aerodrome.financial Phishing (likely) 95/100 · found in CT logs · added by hand

  • TLS certificate issued 2026-10-08 (Certificate Transparency)

Recommended: After client approval: file registrar + host abuse reports, Google Safe Browsing / Netcraft, and wallet blocklist submissions (drafts attached).

Registrar abuse report to Dynadot Inc

File at: abuse@dynadot.com
Subject: Phishing / brand impersonation: aerodrome.financial impersonates Aerodrome

DRAFT - NOT FILED. File only after the brand owner approves it and has signed a Letter of Authorisation.

Hello Dynadot Inc abuse team,

The domain aerodrome[.]financial (registered with you on 2026-04-18) is being used to impersonate Aerodrome, a crypto project whose official site is hxxps://aerodrome[.]finance. We ask you to suspend the domain under your abuse policy and the RAA section 3.18 obligations.

Evidence (observed 2026-11-01, read-only, URLs defanged):
- URL: hxxps://aerodrome[.]financial/
- Hostname contains the brand name
- TLS certificate issued 2026-10-08 (Certificate Transparency)
- Added by hand (--hosts: reported by the client or found by an earlier scan)
- Same favicon as the official site
- Page title resembles the official title (80%)
- Page text overlaps the official site (82% shingles)
- Brand named on the page (6x), including the title
- Wallet-connect prompt/library: connectwallet, walletconnect, Web3Modal
- Drainer-style JS: setApprovalForAll, drainer
- Lure text: rewards
- Resolves to 172.67.199.79, 104.21.21.150
- Page title: "Aerodrome Finance | Liquidity Hub on Base Network"
- Screenshot: shots/aerodrome.financial.jpg (attach)

We are not affiliated with the operator of aerodrome[.]financial. Aerodrome has not authorised its use of the brand. Screenshots and HTML captures are available on request.

Regards,
{{REPORTER_NAME}}
{{REPORTER_ORG}}, acting for Aerodrome under a Letter of Authorisation dated {{LOA_DATE}}
{{REPORTER_EMAIL}}

4. Coverage and limits of this scan

  • Certificate Transparency (crt.sh): aerodrome% 1755 certs; aerodrome.% 1755 certs; aerodrome-% 6 certs; %aerodrome% 6 certs. crt.sh truncates leading-wildcard searches, so names that only contain the brand in the middle can be missed.
  • Newly registered domains (WhoisDS free list, capped at 70k names/day): 2026-10-31: 70000, 2026-10-30: 70000, 2026-10-29: 70000, 2026-10-28: 70000, 2026-10-27: 70000, 2026-10-26: 70000, 2026-10-25: 70000.
  • Tokens: base: 30 days of new pools. Only tokens that got a Uniswap/Aerodrome pool are seen. Free Ethereum RPCs serve about a day of logs.
  • Wallet blocklists checked: metamask, scamsniffer.
  • Not covered yet: X/Twitter, Telegram and Discord impersonators, app stores, paid ads, Solana tokens.
  • Scores are heuristics. Every finding above "low" should be looked at by a person before anything is filed.

5. How this was collected

Only public data was read: DNS, certificate logs, public domain lists, public blockchain logs, RDAP, and the public pages themselves (fetched like a normal visitor; no logins, no clicks, no form input, no wallet connected). No suspect was contacted. Nothing in this report has been filed with any registrar, host or blocklist: every report under "Draft" is text for Aerodrome to approve. We file only with a signed Letter of Authorisation.
